# French review - Flavor Text Extended

Revision: `516e58d` (last commit that touched `Mod/Defs`).

This mod is original: it has no source language other than English, so the Original and English columns carry the same text. It ships no French (the companion mod "Flavor Text Extended - Francais" carries it), so the French column is empty. Grammar tokens such as `{0_coll}` and `{2_plur}` are copied as they ship.

| Key | Original | English | French |
|---|---|---|---|
| FT_Leek.label | Flavor Text leek | Flavor Text leek | |
| FT_Shallot.label | Flavor Text shallot | Flavor Text shallot | |

| FlavorTextFR_MarmiteDieppoiseSolo.label | marmite dieppoise | marmite dieppoise | |
| FlavorTextFR_MarmiteDieppoiseSolo.description | several fish and shellfish poached separately and only then brought together in a broth creamed with cider. Every flesh has its own cooking time: put them all in at once and the whole thing is lost. | several fish and shellfish poached separately and only then brought together in a broth creamed with cider. Every flesh has its own cooking time: put them all in at once and the whole thing is lost. | |
| FlavorTextFR_CanardALaRouennaiseSolo.label | duck à la rouennaise | duck à la rouennaise | |
| FlavorTextFR_CanardALaRouennaiseSolo.description | a bird smothered rather than bled, so as to keep its blood, roasted rare; the carcass is then pressed and the juice bound with the liver and red wine. | a bird smothered rather than bled, so as to keep its blood, roasted rare; the carcass is then pressed and the juice bound with the liver and red wine. | |
| FlavorTextFR_BoudinNoirAuxPommesSolo.label | black pudding with apples | black pudding with apples | |
| FlavorTextFR_BoudinNoirAuxPommesSolo.description | a black pudding pricked with a fork and fried gently — too fierce a heat and it bursts — with apples caramelised in butter. The sugar of the fruit cuts the iron of the blood. | a black pudding pricked with a fork and fried gently — too fierce a heat and it bursts — with apples caramelised in butter. The sugar of the fruit cuts the iron of the blood. | |
| FlavorTextFR_SaladeLyonnaiseSolo.label | salade lyonnaise | salade lyonnaise | |
| FlavorTextFR_SaladeLyonnaiseSolo.description | bitter frisée, lardons fried in the pan, croutons rubbed with garlic and a poached egg set over the top. You break the yolk and it becomes the dressing. | bitter frisée, lardons fried in the pan, croutons rubbed with garlic and a poached egg set over the top. You break the yolk and it becomes the dressing. | |
| FlavorTextFR_PoulardeDemiDeuilSolo.label | poularde demi-deuil | poularde demi-deuil | |
| FlavorTextFR_PoulardeDemiDeuilSolo.description | a fowl under whose skin black slivers of truffle have been slid, poached whole in a broth of bones. The black on the white is what earns it its name: half-mourning. | a fowl under whose skin black slivers of truffle have been slid, poached whole in a broth of bones. The black on the white is what earns it its name: half-mourning. | |
| FlavorTextFR_FocacciaSolo.label | focaccia | focaccia | |
| FlavorTextFR_FocacciaSolo.description | a very wet dough dimpled with the fingertips, drowned in olive oil and salted in coarse grains. The holes hold the oil: they are not decorative. | a very wet dough dimpled with the fingertips, drowned in olive oil and salted in coarse grains. The holes hold the oil: they are not decorative. | |
| FlavorTextFR_UdonSolo.label | udon | udon | |
| FlavorTextFR_UdonSolo.description | thick, soft noodles kneaded underfoot beneath a cloth, in a clear kombu broth. The texture matters more than the taste, and it is deliberate. | thick, soft noodles kneaded underfoot beneath a cloth, in a clear kombu broth. The texture matters more than the taste, and it is deliberate. | |
| FlavorTextFR_MsemenSolo.label | msemen | msemen | |
| FlavorTextFR_MsemenSolo.description | a dough stretched to transparency on an oiled surface, brushed with butter and semolina, folded into a square and folded again, then cooked dry. The layers are counted when it is torn. | a dough stretched to transparency on an oiled surface, brushed with butter and semolina, folded into a square and folded again, then cooked dry. The layers are counted when it is torn. | |
| FlavorTextFR_TarteAuRizSolo.label | rice tart | rice tart | |
| FlavorTextFR_TarteAuRizSolo.description | a very thick rice pudding poured into a case of plain risen dough, then baked until the top crazes over. The speciality of Verviers, eaten there for centuries. | a very thick rice pudding poured into a case of plain risen dough, then baked until the top crazes over. The speciality of Verviers, eaten there for centuries. | |
| FlavorTextFR_NarsharabSolo.label | narsharab | narsharab | |
| FlavorTextFR_NarsharabSolo.description | the Azerbaijani molasses, mounted with sugar and scented with cinnamon, clove and bay. Served with grilled sturgeon, and nothing else will stand in for it. | the Azerbaijani molasses, mounted with sugar and scented with cinnamon, clove and bay. Served with grilled sturgeon, and nothing else will stand in for it. | |
| FlavorTextFR_PongTiaKoonSolo.label | pong tia koon | pong tia koon | |
| FlavorTextFR_PongTiaKoonSolo.description | the Cambodian version, grilled over embers rather than boiled, served with a dip of lime, salt and Kampot pepper. | the Cambodian version, grilled over embers rather than boiled, served with a dip of lime, salt and Kampot pepper. | |
| FlavorTextFR_TortueBraiseeSolo.label | Chinese braised turtle | Chinese braised turtle | |
| FlavorTextFR_TortueBraiseeSolo.description | a soft-shelled turtle braised in rice wine with black mushrooms, ginger and goji berries. The cartilage around the rim is the part sought after, gelatinous and melting. | a soft-shelled turtle braised in rice wine with black mushrooms, ginger and goji berries. The cartilage around the rim is the part sought after, gelatinous and melting. | |
| FlavorTextFR_FabadaSolo.label | fabada | fabada | |
| FlavorTextFR_FabadaSolo.description | large white beans stewed with smoked pork, never stirred with a spoon: the pot is shaken with a turn of the wrist so as not to break them. | large white beans stewed with smoked pork, never stirred with a spoon: the pot is shaken with a turn of the wrist so as not to break them. | |
| FlavorTextFR_FideuaSolo.label | fideuà | fideuà | |
| FlavorTextFR_FideuaSolo.description | short hollow pasta toasted dry then cooked in a fish fumet, which they drink up while standing on end. Born of a ship's boy hiding the rice from a captain who ate too much of it. | short hollow pasta toasted dry then cooked in a fish fumet, which they drink up while standing on end. Born of a ship's boy hiding the rice from a captain who ate too much of it. | |
| FlavorTextFR_TakuanSolo.label | takuan | takuan | |
| FlavorTextFR_TakuanSolo.description | a daikon dried in the sun for weeks until it will bend, then buried in rice bran with sugar and salt. Bright yellow, crisp, and loud to chew. | a daikon dried in the sun for weeks until it will bend, then buried in rice bran with sugar and salt. Bright yellow, crisp, and loud to chew. | |
| FlavorTextFR_SchweinshaxeSolo.label | schweinshaxe | schweinshaxe | |
| FlavorTextFR_SchweinshaxeSolo.description | a whole knuckle roasted for hours and basted with brown beer, until the rind becomes a shell that cracks under the knife. | a whole knuckle roasted for hours and basted with brown beer, until the rind becomes a shell that cracks under the knife. | |
